# The law of the GameBoard

The one document of the law: what the engine computes, in whole numbers,
and why. Every symbol is named in English where it first appears. A
scalar is written plain, a vector in bold lowercase (**n**), a matrix or
an operator in bold uppercase (**C**). A line that no longer holds is
not in this document.

## The GameBoard and the families

### The objects

A **Node** is a physical location. The **GameBoard** is all the Nodes
together, a box of `shape` = (N_x, N_y, N_z) Nodes; each axis is periodic
(the box a torus on that axis) or open (a face beyond which no Node
lies), as the world declares, and a face may be declared receding
(**The unbounded board**, below). Two Nodes that differ by one step along
one axis are joined by a **Link**; each Node has six Links, one through
each of its six **Ports**, named by their outward direction +x, -x, +y,
-y, +z, -z. An axis of one layer folds: its two Ports return the Node
itself. An **inner face** is the face's rule declared inside the board
(the owner's word of 2026-09-30: the wall of the two slits is not matter
but the board's own face): a plane of Nodes across an axis, beyond the
board but for the gaps the world declares; a Node beyond the board is
read as 0 through every Port and reads 0 through its own (a held row
with a declared rest, the massless row at the file's c_vac, read at its
rest there, the vacuum beyond the face being the same vacuum;
**The vacuum content**, [what is open](#what-is-open), item 22), so no
level, current or source crosses its Links, nothing is laid or sourced
there, and no body, message or detector stands there; a declaration of
the file, like the wraps and the detectors' regions, and no number of the
engine. The local information of a Node is its **NodeState**: per
family, per part, two levels and Rule3's remainder, and per held part the
one remainder of its write, and nothing else; its count, its sense, its
currents and its tension are readings of the record
([the count is the record's share](#the-count-is-the-records-share);
[the primitives](#the-primitives), a family's write is one act).
A change of a NodeState is an **Event**; the rule that makes it is a
**LocalRule**, and there is one LocalRule, Rule3 ([Rule3](#rule3)).

**The unbounded board** (the owner, 2026-09-30, via the advisor, "record
it, and also in HIGHLIGHTS and ALGEBRA, so that it enters the paper",
#1519 comment 5907697068, on the advisor's test 5906503409; replacing the
hypothesis The board grows on demand of the same day). Beyond the front
of a world nothing has arrived and every row is 0; a receding face
computes that board only where something is: the board is a dense cube
that grows by a layer of zeros on that side whenever a level other than
0 reaches the Node before the face, so no event ever meets it, and a run
of such a board is a run of the unbounded lattice with 0 beyond the
front, since Rule3 needs nothing else and a level leaves 0 only where a
neighbour was not 0 the interval before. An option beside open, folded
and periodic faces, never the default; the file declares with it a
largest size, and a run whose front reaches that size reports and ends
rather than reflecting; the back-in-time gate is unchanged, the zeros
returning to zeros. On it the massless and the binding rows build their
rest behind the front by the run itself (Huygens on the lattice: a
source switched on reaches its rest within 3 percent in ten intervals;
the advisor's run of one octant with mirror symmetry, 200 intervals,
real arithmetic, Rule3 alone: the massless row [1, 1] at the seed 1,000
at the first interval, 667, 778, 782 at ten intervals, 758 at eighty,
the rest 758 exact; the binding holder [2400, 2401] 780 at ten, 746 at
eighty, its rest 746), the rows with a heavy gap ring and radiate the
ringing away within about a hundred intervals (the polarisation row
[3000, 6000] 736 at the seed at five intervals against its rest 523,
488 at twenty, 525 at eighty, settled by 120, its troughs running
outward, -16 levels at five Links at forty intervals for a source of
1,000 per interval, the troughs that sized c_vac at about 2 percent of
the source's well at five Links, the finding that set the rest of 60 of
the time, 0 since the owner's word of 2026-09-30, 23:15), nothing reflects, the blind numbers of
the looks are the infinite lattice's exactly, and the world's size is
its age in Links. What it does not change: the first act of a world is
the generator's lay, a record with its rows at their rest, since a seed
of one Node crosses the horizon at the second interval and parts its

left at the body; a seed of 500 sheds everything, no body); and the wall

properly", on the build's finding that the far face returns each
quantum's transmitted part through the screen inside the next quanta's
windows); the pixel's and the two slits' worlds and blind rows stand on
their faces as written. What the build found (the engine round of
2026-09-30, `src/event_universe/growth.py`, ENGINE.md): the board grows
by the file's `layers` layers of zeros at a time, a second number of the
face beside its largest size, so the growth copies every array once per
that many intervals per face and holds nothing else (ENGINE.md); the
front is the front of every level of every
family, the held rows' rest fields and their tensions among them, so a
board grows from the binding holder's screened rest, which reaches tens
of Links in integers, before the light arrives (the two slits' bright

first act lays a Node with no level, the levels 0 and every remainder
at its origin (a held row's time part at the start's half wall where the

run of the larger board it grows into bit for bit at every interval
where the rows read 0 at the front at the start (a chain of light alone,

the start the two runs differ by the start alone, the rest being the
declared board's fixed point with its sink at the faces ([what is
open](#what-is-open), item 13), and not by the growth; the end is
lawful and named (the interval, the axis, the side, the largest size)
and the gate goes back over the intervals run; the two slits' bright

to the largest size 8,000 by 64 layers (the train world of that day, gone since with the train,
**The two slits as run are the click experiment**), grown twice, at intervals 51 and 115, to
3,792 along x, said MATCH too over a prefix of 130 intervals. A finding beside
it: a receding face grows the board and stretches nothing, so Hubble's
redshift does not come from the law's growth; if the law is to give it,
it comes from elsewhere, a vacuum content that changes with the age
being the one candidate named, not derived; the two routes by name since
the clusters' cosmology, a kinematic recession on a fixed lattice or
**Gamma is not constant** (**The clusters' cosmology**, [a family's
declaration](#a-familys-declaration)).

A **family** is one kind of physical value with its own declarations
([a family's declaration](#a-familys-declaration)). A family's **record** is a set of levels over the
GameBoard, two levels per Node (now and before) with a remainder per Node;
the family's level at a Node is one number.
A **body** is a set of Nodes on which a family's **count** stands, with
the values the body's writer declares (its content, its momentum, its
spin, its moment); a body is one connected region. A **message** is a
record the world lays and no body: a packet of a family of quanta, its
axis, its wave number, its amplitude and its envelope declared in the
file and its levels the generator's ([the generator](#the-generator) (h)).

### A family's declaration

Every physical number comes from the world's files: the universe file
(the families and the universe's integers) and the world file (the
bodies, the detectors, the readings). The engine holds no number of its
own; a scale that is no physics (the amplitude unit A of [the generator](#the-generator) (f), a
product bound) is derived from the integer width, never written.

The universe's integers: the Node clock Gamma (the pace of an empty
Node, every family's clock unit); the quantum's action T (`quantum_action`),
the action of one quantum of any family, a unit as Gamma is; the momentum unit Q_unit; and the vacuum content c_vac, the level at which the massless row rests at the faces, and everywhere where no write is declared, a key of that row in the universe file, 0 by the owner's word (**The vacuum content**, [what is open](#what-is-open), item 22; the owner's word of 2026-09-30, 23:15, #1563 comment 5921436204), the faces' content the positive bound U_faces >= Lambda R^2 / 6 where the vacuum's write is declared, the write's number one more declaration of the file (**The vacuum writes**, the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 19:22).

**The families from the rule** (the owner's question, 2026-09-28, 09:13 Israel: how are the families derived from Rule3): a family is a band of the one rule and not a declaration, and its row holds nothing the rule and the geometry fix. Its pair [num, den], cos omega_0 = num / den: the vacuum's band [1, 1] (the rule's own massless line) and the exact band [1, 2] (2 cos omega_0 = 1, the period 6, one of the three exact rotations the rule allows, whose records at rest never click, the current 0) are the rule's; the matter pair (the word "body", every body its own) is the one free physical pair, and beside it, by the owner's word of 2026-09-30 (#1519 comment 5904935342, "go with it, with the caveat that we may have been wrong"), the file declares, **The two rows**, the binding holder's gapped pair [num_b, den_b], num_b < den_b (the first [2400, 2401]): a fourth family, a holder of the content at the level weight 1, stepped by Rule3 at its pair as the vacuum's row is, its rest the screened sum with the range R = sqrt(num_b / (6 (den_b - num_b))) Links (20 at [2400, 2401]), the law's strong force; and gravity's level weight LW (the quanta of form that write one level of content, LW T the measure of a level as W_c = 3 den T is the quantum's measure of a current; the owner's name of 2026-10-01), the row of the file (**The vacuum's row steps at the divisor 1**).

The families as the rule derives them from the two keys of a row, its pair and what it holds, with the reads on the held row's pair, a row its pair, what it holds and its level weight and nothing else, no row declaring whether it clicks (the owner, 2026-09-30, via the advisor, "without exception, obviously", #1515 comment 5910444380) (**The families from the rule** above; the rows of the rule's own universe, `examples/events/rule.json`, and the third a band the rule allows and the universe does not use, **The third and the colours, closed as absent**):

**Every family has a dimension** (the owner, 2026-10-01, 02:55 and 03:10, via the advisor, "is the key not by the family's dimension?", "yes", "every family has a dimension", #1563 comments 5923616421, 5923771616 and 5924079542; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the line of the same name): the dimension is the family's declaration in the universe file, per family and per run, never a reading of a Node's own state and never a fact of one lay. A family of quanta declares it, the key `dimension`, 1 (one real line) or 2 (a plane, re and im: charged matter); a held row declares no shape but what sources it, the key `sources` of its `held` key (`form`, the time line; `form` and `tensions`, the time line and the three axis lines; `wronskian`, the time line), its shape its sources' count, one real line per source, and a held row is never a plane, since a plane's own W would be its own source. The engine steps every line as a line of dimension one and knows nothing of a family but its pair and its shape, so every rule is a function of these with no name. What a record can source is what its parts afford, one real line the form D and no W, a plane the Wronskian W, real lines the tensions; by the hold's reciprocity what a family reads is what it sources, so a held row sourced by W holds the sign, a held row sourced by D and the tensions holds the content, a plane reads the holder of the sign and a real line does not (neutral by itself), everyone reads the content, a held row of the content its own level among it, at the Node's pace (**Every row reads the content**, [the paces](#the-paces), the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 15:32; the exception "a held row of the content reads nothing, and no row its own level" left with it): light is the holder of the sign's own real record, dimension 1 ([the rows against nature](#the-rows-against-nature) (g)), so it reads no holder of the sign, a static charge does not bend light, the holder of the sign does not couple to its own sign and light's W is 0, no charge. A neutral body and a charged body of the same pair are two families, two rows with the same pair and the dimensions 1 and 2, and never one family laid two ways; the mode file lays within the family's dimension, a second level pair on a family of dimension one refused by name. The held key holds the sources and the numbers, `level_weight` and `rest`. The holder's act on its readers is its row's declaration, the key `act` of its `held` key: the plain read into the paces, every holder's without the key, or the rotation of the two-part record, the holder of the sign's alone, under which it carries three odd axis lines beside its time line (**The sign holder rotates the two-part record**, [the hypotheses under their own names](#the-hypotheses-under-their-own-names); the rotation round). The dimension has a meaning in clicks: the number of independent readings a detector can take of the family (a count; a count and a sense; a count and three tensions; the joint shares of a pair). The two experiments' worlds are unchanged, matter of dimension one reading nothing of the holder and no W sourced (the advisor's table, 5924079542):

**The mass is one integer** (the owner, 2026-09-28, 10:46 Israel): every pair is written over the GameBoard's one denominator Gamma, cos omega_0 = m / Gamma, the file holding the numerator m alone: light m = Gamma, the exact band m = Gamma div 2 (Gamma even), matter its one integer, 4,000 over 6,000 in the rule's own universe (2 / 3 exactly); **the matter pair's den is the family's own integer, not Gamma** (the owner's conditional word of 2026-10-01, 09:25, via the advisor, "go for it if it resolves contradictions", #1563 comment 5925957569, in force on the two hands, the mathematician's #1572 comment 5926507856 and the advisor's #1563 comment 5926948607): Rule3 takes any [num, den] and the binding holder's 2,401 already is its own, so the reading of this line as m over Gamma is replaced, **The wall is one** keeping the pair as the file writes it, w = 6 den Gamma^2 for every family, no code change and the two gates untouched; den = Gamma is excluded by nature's two clicks, alpha and Cassini's gamma (Gamma at most 401 and gamma_K - 1 = 1 / (den - 1) at least 1 / 400 = 2.5 x 10^-3, a hundred standard deviations from Cassini; [the constants](#the-constants), **The law's alpha is a reading of Gamma sin omega_0**);

**The wall is one** (Cheshbon's correction, 2026-09-28, 14:12 Israel, on the de Broglie Experimenter's finding that a pair reduced to lowest terms shrinks the wall 4,000-fold, lifts A to 1.3 x 10^9 and drives the count's line past the width): the wall is w = 6 Gamma^3 for every family, the pair enters the coefficients as the file writes it, num over den, the den the family's own integer and not Gamma since the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 09:25 (**The mass is one integer**; m over Gamma until it) (R_a = 2 m p_a^2, the rest term 12 (Gamma - m) p_0^2), and a reduction to lowest terms is a reading and never the loader's act, so every family's remainder has the one resolution and the exact bands leave none in any form (S + 6 R = 6 Gamma^3 exactly); so the width's amplitude unit falls (A = 512,409 in the universe of record against 4,270,079 at [800, 1200], the pair (level, remainder) carrying the same bits) and T is set so that every record at c T stays under A (T at most 9 x 10^11 for the givers of record; 5 x 10^11 proposed).

**The final unification** (the owner, 2026-09-28, 10:53 Israel; the level weights, the reads, the sign and the parts as the owner's words of 2026-09-30 have them): the three of Rule3 is the three axes; the three axes give the three tensions a held row carries beside its time part, the axis contents Rule3 reads into the Link's paces, and no other part: the parts Rule3 never reads (a vector part, the off-diagonal parts of a tensor) are not in the law (the owner, 2026-09-30, "the parts Rule3 never reads leave"); every family carries two keys, its pair and its level weight: the field on the pace's own band [1, 1] holding the content carries the time part and the three tensions (gravity, 1 + 3), the holder of the sign on [1, 1] its time part alone (the charge, a scalar whose quanta are light), a quantized scalar holds nothing and has no level weight (matter, its pair the mass m), and the binding holder, a gapped pair holding the content at the level weight 1, is the law's strong force (**The two rows**, the owner, 2026-09-30, #1519 comment 5904935342); in the rule's own universe every level weight is 1 but gravity's LW (**The rule's own universe**), so the parts carry no free number beside LW, and the free pairs are the mass and the binding holder's. The crack, named: a second massive scalar family, a second mass, the rule does not forbid, and nature's spectrum of masses is the open row under this line. Every family's record carries a count and its current, the holders' included, and no row declares whether it clicks (the owner, 2026-09-30, via the advisor, "without exception, obviously", #1515 comment 5910444380): a static field never clicks by the arithmetic, a static record having now = next = before, its form D = 0 and its current F = 0 at every Link, so the massless row at its rest and a body's static well carry no quanta, while a held row's travelling events carry a count, the share of their record, entering a detector like light's ([the count is the record's share](#the-count-is-the-records-share)); the massless row and the exact band that hold the content are two real fields; a family that carries quanta and holds the sign is one record, its time part its real level pair (**Light is born by the write**); an exact band's record (the third on [-1, 2]) clicks as every family's does, exactly only in the vacuum (**The exact bands** below). Its reads ([the paces](#the-paces)): every family reads every holder of the content at the weight 1, the held rows of the content among the readers, each its own level among it (**Every row reads the content**), a row without a gap and, since **The two rows** (5904935342), a row with a gap holding the content alike as its stepped field (**The vacuum's row steps at the divisor 1**), and, where its dimension is 2 (a plane), every holder of the sign plainly (**Every family has a dimension**; light, the holder's own real record, reads none); a family sources what it reads at the weight it reads with. The action of one quantum is T, the universe's one integer for every family: Rule3 is linear and its form's scale is free, so no line of Rule3 fixes T; it is a unit, declared once, and no family, no body and no emitter declares its own (the owner, 2026-09-28). So the files hold Gamma, T, the matter pair, the binding holder's pair, gravity's level weight LW (**The two rows**, 5904935342) and the vacuum content c_vac, the massless row's rest (**The vacuum content**, the owner's word of 2026-09-30, 5906289037), every other divisor 1, and nothing else of physics.

**The exact bands** (the owner's word, 2026-09-28, 11:44 Israel: the quarks are there for a reason and are almost never seen; the algebra on one Node, 12:03 Israel): the line of one Node at rest, a_next = 2 cos omega_0 a_now - a_before, is exact in integers only where 2 cos omega_0 is an integer, so the rule carries exactly three exact rotations, 2 cos omega_0 = 1, 0 and -1, the periods 6, 4 and 3, the pairs [1, 2], [0, 1] and [-1, 2]; a record on an exact band at rest at the vacuum's pace leaves no remainder in its rest rotation, so its quanta stand exactly at every Node, it gives nothing and never decays alone, and stable matter is built on an exact band; everything else of it clicks as every family's does, its packets (a wave number is no integer rotation), its Nodes in a well (2 cos omega = 2 - (p_0 / Gamma)^2 there) and its reads, so an exact band is seen exactly when it is moved or probed and only then (the owner's word, 2026-09-28, 12:25 Israel: it does click).

**The exact bands are exact only in the vacuum** (Cheshbon's correction, 2026-09-28, 13:09 Israel): at a Node of pace p_0 the band's rest rotation is 2 cos omega = 2 - 2 (den - num) (p_0 / Gamma)^2, an integer at p_0 = Gamma alone, so a record on an exact band inside a well, its own or another's, leaves a remainder and clicks like every family's: the third's three phases sum not to 0 but to a share of a record's level that grows with the well, 1 + 2 cos omega' of a record's level with 2 cos omega' = 2 - 3 (p_0 / Gamma)^2 at [-1, 2] and k = 0: 0.015 at a level 30 of 12,000, 0.146 at 300, 1.05 at the self-binding edge and 1.5 at the horizon, and white matter stands only where the well at its Nodes is small against Gamma: under the level weight 1 no white pixel exists and the third and the polarisation are messages of the vacuum; a white body would need a level weight far above 1, which the universe does not have (a finding). The first is the bound charge, the polarisation, never seen free.

**The third and the colours, closed as absent** (the advisor on the owner's word of 2026-09-30, #1519 comment 5903952622; the standing line **The colours are the three axes** of 2026-09-28 kept): the third [-1, 2] is a band the rule allows and the universe does not use, the mirror of [1, 2] (**A pair's numerator may be negative** below), and one family on it holds no standing body: its read is negative, so its own well pushes its rest rotation up into the vacuum's band (2 cos omega = 0.31 at a count 3,000 of 12,000, inside [-1, 1]) and the record leaks, a record of the third at one Node being a packet of every wave number that disperses in every universe, its uniform mode alone exact (**A third does not bind itself**). The three colours were the count's line's three tallies sigma_x, sigma_y, sigma_z, a detector's reading of the click's axis, no charge of the body and no force of their own; whiteness is their balance at rest (an isotropic tail carries no net current through any Port). The law's strong force is the binding holder's screened sum with the range R, 20 Links at [2400, 2401] (**The two rows**, the owner, 2026-09-30, #1519 comment 5904935342; until it the law had no strong force but the tail's reach), and a nucleus is bodies within R, the cluster line re-read; beside it the tail: under a pull the clicks are biased along one axis at the rate omega_b e^(-kappa d) per interval, which falls with the distance and gives no linear potential, so the force between two pixels through their tails is short-ranged, its range 1 / kappa (2.2 Links at 3,000 of 12,000; the click's reach 18 Links at T = 1); a pixel has no inside, and the smallest bound fraction of a horizon pixel is 0.2255 / (1 / 2) = 0.451, so a third of a pixel never binds and disperses (confinement is the edge, no line of its own). So the quarks are not in the law: the white body, the tube, the string, the charges 2 / 3 and 1 / 3, the generations and up and down leave with the third, no line and no look, and the band [0, 1] of the period 4 is the same, a band the rule allows and the universe does not use. In the rule's own universe the matter is the pixels of [2, 3] and the light, and the third and the polarisation are messages, exact in the vacuum and clicking in every well (**The exact bands are exact only in the vacuum**).

**The conversion** (a hypothesis under its own name: the weak force): a click that takes a quantum of one family and gives it as quanta of others, the rotations adding, is the whole weak interaction, one Node's act, no field and no massive vector, and no family's row declares anything for it. The charges 2 / 3 and 1 / 3, the generations and the band [0, 1] are no rows under it: they left with the third (**The third and the colours, closed as absent**).

### The interval

The engine steps every family from the state at the interval's start to
the state at its end. Every read is of the start's values: the six
neighbours' levels, the Node's own pace, the arrivals, never a value
written in the same interval; one Link per interval and nothing in zero
time. Before the first interval the whole initial state is checked once
against the guard ([the paces](#the-paces), **The guard**), and no act of
the interval reads a Node to refuse. The interval has four acts, in order:

(i) the read: every family's paces from the held families' levels at the start, the reading row's own among them, the clock p_0 = Gamma (1 - 1 / Gamma)^c and the Link's pace p_0^2 / Gamma (**The paces compose**, [the paces](#the-paces)), and the Link's factor from the Link's tension, one number per Link (**The interval's dependency radius is one Link** below); (ii) Rule3 on every record: each family of quanta's two level pairs by its pair and its paces (the holder of the sign's time part is its real pair and steps so, bent by the content it reads), each held row's parts by its pair and its paces, its own level among the content it reads (**Every row reads the content**, [the paces](#the-paces); at the pace 1, reading nothing, until the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 15:32); (iii) the
readings of every family's record: its six currents at the pair the
step started from and its tension's part on each axis at the Node,
-num h_a(i) with h_a(i) = now_(i-a) now_(i+a) - now_i^2 at the levels
now, both read before Rule3 acts, its form and its Wronskian about the
step, and the detectors' reports from the currents, per family and declared region the net inflow through the
region's front boundary Ports, the click, reported with the region and

and (SUM_f w_f (-num_f h_a,f) + r_aa) div (E_s W_c) for each axis part, each Node's own part of the sources' stresses, minus h (**The tension**), one remainder per part
(a row with a gap steps and takes its write as the vacuum's row does, since **The two rows**); light is born here, by the write of a
rotating body's varying Wronskian into the sign holder's record.

**The interval's dependency radius is one Link** (the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 13:25, via the advisor, #1563 comments 5929467347 to 5929505765, on the advisor's reading, 5929405654 item 5 and #1538 comment 5929397155, that the engine read the stresses two Links out from the stepped record and wrote them in the same interval, so a held row's axis part after one interval depended on the state three Links away): every booking of the interval is read from the record as it stands at the interval's start and reaches the Node's six neighbours through its Ports and no further, each Node sourcing its own part of the tension, -num h_a(i) with h_a(i) = now_(i-a) now_(i+a) - now_i^2, into the held row's axis part, the Link's tension being its two ends' parts, one number per Link read the same from both ends by the pace at the next interval and never per Node per axis (**The tension**, [the primitives](#the-primitives); the advisor, #1563 comment 5931460756); so a Node's NodeState after one interval depends on the state at the Node and its six neighbours alone, and a change of the state two Links away leaves it bit for bit, forward and back, the test on every pull request (`tests/test_the_node.py::test_every_act_of_the_interval_reaches_one_link`); status: the law, the owner's word; fence: GameBoard.

Backward, the acts run by Rule3's direction -1: a held row's hold back
once every family that sources it is booked back, a family of quanta
booked back (its records back, its form and its Wronskian read from them) once its own write is off,
so the holder of the sign goes before the rows it sources; every interval
returns bit for bit (**The inverse** of [the count is the record's share](#the-count-is-the-records-share);
**Clicks go only forward; the backward run is exact**), and only the
lay is not taken back. The back-in-time gate (the owner's word of
2026-09-30) is the whole run: N intervals forward and N back, every
array of every family compared at every interval on the way back, bit
for bit, MATCH or MISS by name; a held row with a gap steps and
inverts by Rule3's own direction since **The two rows** (until it, it
held one level and no past, the well laid this interval, so the back
step returned it only where the wells that source it stood still between
two intervals; [what is open](#what-is-open), item 21).

### Readings and measurements

A **measurement** is a detector's click: the detector's report of one
interval, the net current into the region of Nodes the file declares as
the detector through its front boundary Ports, in the current's units
([the count is the record's share](#the-count-is-the-records-share)), for
every family, the detector reporting the region and the family and never a
Node
(**No click names a Node**), one quantum credited to one detector by
the shares, the host's reading (**The click is the detector's report**);
nothing ends and nothing is handed over. Only a measurement is compared
with nature, clicks against clicks, and what produces a click is not
known and not claimed (the owner, 2026-09-30, #1538 comment
5912097355). A **GameBoard reading** (a level, a support, a total, a
body's centre, a count at a Node) is a diagnostic and is labelled so
wherever it appears; a number whose kind is not named is not a result. Displays read state and write nothing.
Every run is headless; the readings are declared in the world file and
written in one format, each labelled DETECTOR, GAMEBOARD or HOST.

## Rule3

### The line

Rule3 is named for the three axes of the lattice, the three directions of the vector the rule works with: the Link's paces (p_x, p_y, p_z) are that vector, and the line has one read coefficient per direction, R_a = 2 num p_a^2, serving the direction's two Ports; the six Ports and the wall's 6 in w = 6 den Gamma^2 are the three directions twice, so the six follows from the three; in time the rule is a three-level recurrence (before, now, next), and in light's own line, num = den at the vacuum's paces, the three stands in the open: a_next = the six arrivals over 3 minus a_before.

**The law in one line**: at every Node, each interval, the division act below with its coefficients from the family's pair and the Node's paces; every level on the GameBoard is written by that same act ([a family's write](#the-primitives)), every count is a reading of the record, its share over the wall W_c ([the count is the record's share](#the-count-is-the-records-share)), and everything else is a number in a file. Every family, at every Node, with the wall w, the read coefficient R_a on
each axis a, the self coefficient S, the level a_now at the interval's
start, a_before one interval earlier, and the remainder r kept at the Node:

  w a_next + r' = SUM_a R_a (arr_(+a) + arr_(-a)) + S a_now - w a_before + r,
  0 <= r' < w,

arr_(±a) the **arrival** through the Port ±a: the neighbour's level; 0 beyond an open face;
the Node's own level on a folded axis. The remainder r is the Node's own
and never travels.

The coefficients at a Node, from its paces ([the paces](#the-paces)) and its
family's pair [num, den]:

  w   = 6 den Gamma^2,
  R_a = 2 num p_a^2,
  S   = 12 den Gamma^2 - 12 (den - num) p_0^2 - 4 num (p_x^2 + p_y^2 + p_z^2).

At the vacuum's pace p_0 = p_a = Gamma the line is the plain second-order
rule of the pair: a_next + a_before = 2 cos omega a_now with the
six-neighbour coupling, the rotation omega_0 with cos omega_0 = num /
den at wave number zero. A held family's field steps at its row's pair at the Node's pace, its own level among the content it reads (**Every row reads the content**, [the paces](#the-paces)), the wall 3 den at the vacuum's pace, at first order.
**The clock once and the Link twice**, the two paces in one line: from the coefficients above, the rotation at a Node of the clock's pace p_0 and the Link's paces p_a is cos omega = 1 - (1 - num / den) (p_0 / Gamma)^2 - (num / (3 den)) SUM_a (p_a / Gamma)^2 (1 - cos k_a), the mass term scaling with the clock's square and the band term with the Link's pace. Two readings follow and no third.

(i) **The clocks shift alike**: at k = 0 every massive band's rest rotation obeys 1 - cos omega_b = (p_0 / Gamma)^2 (1 - cos omega_0), one factor for every family, so two bound bodies at one Node shift their clocks alike (the redshift Phi = c / Gamma to the first order) and, since the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 17:02, every bound line with them (**The same clock and the same ruler for every act**, [the paces](#the-paces)), and a packet's fall in a pace gradient, Delta x |d omega_b / d c| x (c_+ - c_-) / D, does not depend on its family beyond the band's own Delta and d omega_b / d c (item 3 of [what is open](#what-is-open)).

(ii) **The Link is twice**: along a Link every record's speed falls with p_a = (p_0^2 / Gamma) (Gamma - t_a) / Gamma, Gamma - 2 c - t_a to first order, the level entering the Link twice and the clock once, the Node's own content read twice, the Link the clock twice (**The paces compose**, [the paces](#the-paces); the two-end pace of the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 10:45, p_a(i, j) = Gamma - c_i - c_j - t_a, replaced by the word of 15:32, **The clock is the Node's, the tension is the Link's**, [the paces](#the-paces)), so light, which reads the vacuum's row as every family does, is delayed and bent into a mass by twice the clock's share (the bending 4 U_1 / b, [the rows against nature](#the-rows-against-nature) (b)). The line is not conformal: a light record at a fixed wave number scales with (p_a / Gamma)^2 and a bound mode at rest with (p_0 / Gamma)^2, and the two differ already at the first order in c / Gamma (0.625 against 0.411 at c = 3,000 of 12,000 on [8000, 12000], k = 0 against pi); a light record's rotation is conserved along its path, the paces being static, so a light record read against a matter clock at one level reads the clock's shift, (i), and the bending reads (ii).

### The direction

Let sigma be the direction, +1 forward and -1 backward, and let div and
mod be the floor division and its remainder in [0, w), and rho the
remainder carried in. With

  u = sigma (SUM_a R_a arr_a + S x - w y) + rho,
  z = sigma (u div w),  rho' = u mod w,

forward (x, y, rho) = (a_now, a_before, r) gives (z, rho') = (a_next, r');
backward (x, y, rho) = (a_now, a_next, r') gives (z, rho') = (a_before, r).

**Proof**. Forward, sigma = 1, the line is the definition of div and mod.
Backward, sigma = -1: write Y for the right side's first two terms, SUM_a
R_a arr_a + S a_now. Then u = w a_next - Y + r', and z = -floor(u / w) =
ceil((Y - w a_next - r') / w). From the forward line, Y - w a_next - r' = w
a_before - r with 0 <= r < w, whose ceiling divided by w is a_before
exactly; and rho' = u - w floor(u / w) = w a_before - (Y - w a_next - r') =
r. So the backward call returns (a_before, r) bit for bit; the reversibility
of every run needs no second function. Checked bit for bit on 20,000 random
cases against a forward and a backward function written separately.

### The four acts

Rule3's line is one act; declared coefficients make it four:

(a) **The read**, the line above over the GameBoard (the six arrivals); (b) **The one-Node step**, the line with the neighbours' reads declared 0: a_next +
a_before = (a / b) a_now, the rotation by the angle whose doubled cosine is
a / b (Chebyshev's recurrence); with a / b = 1 and the coefficient on
a_before declared 0 it keeps a level; with a / b = 2 from (0, 1) it counts,
a_t = t; (c) **The division**, the line with R = S = 0: w a_next + r' = the
numerator + r, Euclid's division with the remainder kept; (d) **The load**, a
declared integer in the numerator, how a count enters a level.

**A composition** of Rule3's acts with declared coefficients is a finite
sequence of these acts, each with its coefficients (a pair, a weight, a
wall, a load) declared in the files, applied to declared levels.

**Theorem** (what the acts can form). Every composition of the four acts is
a piecewise-linear function of the levels with integer slopes: a sum of
linear forms and of floors of linear forms, composed. No such function
is quadratic in the levels.

**Proof**. Each act is a linear form of the levels or the floor of one; a
floor of a linear form is piecewise linear with integer slopes; sums and
compositions of piecewise-linear functions with integer slopes are of
the same kind. The second difference of such a function in any one
level is 0 almost everywhere, while the second difference of a product
now_i before_j in the pair (now_i, before_j) is 1. So nothing bilinear
comes from the acts alone.

What the theorem separates: every product of two levels in the engine
is a **booking**, a reading of a bilinear form with declared
coefficients and no write ([the booking](#the-booking)); the one thing written from a
product is a held row's source (the write of the form, the Wronskian's quanta and the tension, [the primitives](#the-primitives)); the count is a product read and never written ([the count is the record's share](#the-count-is-the-records-share)), and its change is Rule3's own conserved current.

**The three tests of every rule**. A line enters the law only if it is
generic (one primitive with declared integers, no family's name and no
kind), vector (one of the acts on the state, no root and no float) and
local (its own record and the six neighbours, nothing kept at a Node
beyond the law's numbers). A hypothesis that needs more is stated under
its own name, outside the law.

### The paces

The **pace** of a Node for a family is what its reads make of the Node
clock:

  p_0 = Gamma (1 - 1 / Gamma)^c to the unit, the clock, Gamma e^(-c / Gamma) to O(1 / Gamma), its square Gamma^2 - 2 Gamma c + 2 c^2 to the second order (**The paces compose** below),  c = SUM over the reads of (weight x the read family's time level at the Node), the reading row's own level among them where it holds the content,
  p_a(i, j) x p_a(i, j) = p_i^2 q_ij^2 / Gamma^2,  p_i = p_0(i)^2 / Gamma,  q_ij = Gamma - t_a(i, j),  a = x, y, z, the Link from the Node i to its neighbour j through the Port a, p_i the Node's pace, the clock twice (Gamma - 2 c_i to first order, **The paces compose**), its own content c_i read twice, and q_ij the Link's factor from the Link's tension t_a(i, j), one number per Link from the read families' axis parts at its two ends, read the same from both ends (**The interval's dependency radius is one Link**, [the interval](#the-interval); **The tension**, [the primitives](#the-primitives)); the product and not the sum, Gamma - 2 c_i - t_a(i, j) to first order and Gamma - 2 c in a uniform level with no tension (**Every row reads the content** and **The clock is the Node's, the tension is the Link's** below),

one division per read per Link, rounded at the read: the pace is a coefficient of the interval and no level, so no remainder is kept for it (nothing at a Node but the law's numbers). A positive weight is a hollow (the read family's level
slows the clock, an attraction); a negative weight is a hill. The reads are the family's dimension's (**Every family has a dimension**, [a family's declaration](#a-familys-declaration)): every family reads every holder of the content, the held rows among the readers, a holder of the content its own level among it (**Every row reads the content** below), a family of dimension 2 (a plane) reads every holder of the sign plainly (light, the holder's own real record of dimension 1, reads none), and no weight and no dimension is read from a Node's own state, the files declare them. A family with no reads steps at p_0 = p_a = Gamma, the plain rule. The clock's slowing
is this pace: a record at a Node of pace p rotates and moves as a record
at Gamma does, with its intervals p / Gamma as long.

### The bound

The rule's total at a Node whose levels and arrivals stand at the
amplitude A is at most 6 A |R| + A |S| + w (A + 1). Every world declares
integers under which this total sits below 2^width - 1, the width the
universe file declares (the engine's integers are the hardware's 64-bit
integers at a width at or under 63 and Python's integers above it, the
loader's one choice at the start, ENGINE.md), or the loader refuses it naming the total; the
generator's amplitude unit is the largest A that keeps it inside
([the generator](#the-generator) (f)). With Gamma = 10^4 and A = 2^20 the total is near 3 x
10^18, one third of the room.

### The self-source

The **self-source** of a family with the unit P_2
above 0, at every Node from the start's levels,

  Sigma_self = (SUM over the six Links, the family's records and their levels of (a_j - a_i)^2) div P_2,

is subtracted from the step's right side as w Sigma_self: each
difference is a read act, its square a booking of the family's own
levels, the division Rule3's with the remainder not kept; P_2 = 0 turns
the line off, and a nonzero P_2 stands at or above 24 A (the generator's unit).

### The conserved form

The read matrix 2 num p_i^2 q_ij^2 / Gamma^2 is not symmetric where the paces differ, so
the form carries the weight 1 / p_i^2 on the Node terms and the Link's
factor squared, q_ij^2 / Gamma^2, on the current of each Link:

  E = SUM_i [w (now_i^2 + before_i^2) - S_i now_i before_i] / p_i^2 - 2 num SUM_i SUM_(j ~ i) (q_ij^2 / Gamma^2) now_i before_j,

the inner sum over the six neighbours j of the Node i and q_ij = Gamma - t_a(i, j) the Link's factor from its tension; the plain Link term, SUM_i now_i S_6(before)_i with S_6 the sum over the six neighbours, holds at q_ij = Gamma only (the automated reviewer's #1599; the mathematician's 74 C, #1572 comment 5936595203, by exact fractions on the reviewer's periodic three-Node board, the plain form moving from 96 to 129 in one step and the weighted form standing; the advisor's second hand, #1563 comment 5936942630; two hands; the engine's weighted form carries it). E is exact where the paces stand and changes by the work term where they move; it is a GameBoard reading. The clock is the Node's and the tension is the Link's (the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 15:32, [the paces](#the-paces)): the Node's weight 1 / p_i^2 is the Node's pace from its own content twice, p_i = p_0(i)^2 / Gamma, the clock twice (Gamma - 2 c_i to first order, **The paces compose**), and not the clock's p_0 (the mathematician's second hand, #1572 comment 5932007026, item 47), and the Link's coefficient is the Node's factor times the Link's own factor q_ij^2, the same from both ends (**The interval's dependency radius is one Link**, [the interval](#the-interval)), so the form is exact for any field and any tensions, before the integer remainders (Theorem 3 of the paper, restated so; the structure's conservation checked in real arithmetic, the drift 9 x 10^-13 over 400 steps with random weights and 1.2 x 10^-15 with the product on the advisor's ring, and not conserved with the tension booked per Node per axis, as the engine booked it before the locality round, nor with the tension added into the pace, the sum (Gamma - 2 c_i - t_a(i, j))^2, whose drift is 3 x 10^-3; the blind the drift 10^-15 with tensions present).
Its Link term is the current num (now_i before_j - before_i now_j)
through the Link ij, the current of Rule3's own conservation law and
the one product the click reads ([the count is the record's share](#the-count-is-the-records-share)), carried in the form with the Link's factor squared q_ij^2 / Gamma^2, 1 where no tension stands.

**Proof** at constant paces. Without the rounding the line is a_next +
a_before = **M** a_now with **M** the matrix whose diagonal is S_i / w
and whose entries on the six Links are R / w. Let **D** be the diagonal matrix of the weights 1 / p_i^2, p_i = p_0(i)^2 / Gamma, Gamma - 2 c_i to first order; then **D M** = **W** is symmetric where the Link's coefficient at the reading Node is the Node's factor times the Link's own factor, R_a(i -> j) = 2 num p_i^2 q_ij^2 / Gamma^2 with q_ij = q_ji = Gamma - t_a(i, j) from the Link's tension (the structure **D**^-1 **W**, [the paces](#the-paces)), the weight 1 / p_i^2 cancelling the Node's factor on both sides of every Link; where the tension is booked per Node per axis, or added into the pace as (Gamma - 2 c_i - t_a(i, j))^2, the two sides differ, no Node diagonal exists and the form drifts (the advisor, #1563 comment 5932163303). For a symmetric **K** = **D M** the quantity
Q(x, y) = x . **D** x + y . **D** y - x . **K** y satisfies Q(a_next,
a_now) = Q(a_now, a_before): with a_next = **M** a_now - a_before,
a_next . **D** a_next - a_next . **K** a_now = (**M** a_now - a_before)
. **D** (**M** a_now - a_before) - (**M** a_now - a_before) . **D M**
a_now = a_before . **D** a_before - a_before . **D M** a_now, which
is the other side. Multiplied by w this is E up to the Link term's
sign convention. With the rounding, each Node's remainder adds at most

## The click

### The booking

The **current** of a record into Node i from its neighbour j through
their Link over an interval is the bilinear form of its two levels at
the two ends,

  F_ij = weight x (now_i before_j - before_i now_j),

the weight the family's num; positive inward: a
record of wave number k travelling from i to j has F_ij = -2 weight x
|psi|^2 sin omega sin k, below 0. It is antisymmetric, F_ji = -F_ij, and
it is the Link term of the conserved form of [the conserved form](#the-conserved-form): the current of
Rule3's own conservation law. The booking reads it at a Port and writes
nothing.

### The count is the record's share

Every family's **count** is a reading of its record and no line of its
own (the owner, 2026-09-30, 23:55, "approved: throw out the count's line
and the sense's line", on the Boss's answer that no act of Rule3 reads
them; HIGHLIGHTS.md, **The count's line and the sense's line leave the
law**): the record's weighted share at every Node,

  e_i = 3 [w (now_i^2 + before_i^2) - S_i now_i before_i] div (SUM over the six Ports of p_a(i, j)^2) - num x now_i x S_6(before)_i,

the conserved form's Node term over twice the mean of the six Links' paces
squared less the plain Link term ([the conserved form](#the-conserved-form)),
with p_a(i, j) the pace of the Link through the Port, p_i q_ij / Gamma, the Node's pace p_0(i)^2 / Gamma times the Link's factor Gamma - t_a(i, j) over Gamma, Gamma - 2 c_i - t_a(i, j) to first order (**The clock is the Node's, the tension is the Link's** and **The paces compose**, [the paces](#the-paces)),
its tension one number per Link read the same from both ends ([the interval](#the-interval)),
2 SUM_a p_a^2 where each axis's two Links share a pace, and S_6 the sum over the
six Ports, at the paces of the read, in the current's units, Rule3's
integers exact at any size; the share of a family's two level pairs (re, im)
is the sum of the two. In **quanta** it is read over W_c, the quantum's
measure of a current (**The click is the detector's report** below), by the
division act, (e_i + W_c div 2) div W_c, the nearest whole: the unit every
book, every declaration and every credit is read in. Nothing is kept at a
Node beside the record: no count, no remainder of a count, no sense. What
stays is Rule3 on the record and four readings of it: D, the form, the wells'
source; W, the Wronskian, the rotation sense, the sign holder's source; F,
the current, what a detector reads through its boundary
Ports; and the tension from the levels into the paces.

**The share's change is the currents** (the paper writer's finding, #1538
comment 5921398465; the Boss's check, 5921607048; the advisor on the
anisotropy, 5909822494). Over one step of Rule3 the share at the paces the
step read changes by exactly

  e_i(next, now) - e_i(now, before) = SUM_j F_ij + num (next_i - before_i) SUM_j (6 p_a(i, j)^2 - P^2) now_j / P^2 - 3 (next_i - before_i) (r'_i - r_i) / P^2,

F_ij = num (now_i before_j - before_i now_j) the six currents into the Node
at the pair the step started from ([the booking](#the-booking)), P^2 =
SUM over the six Ports of p_a(i, j)^2, now_j the arrival through each, r and r' Rule3's
remainder before and after the step: the first term the currents, the second
the paces' anisotropy (0 where the six paces are equal, the one pace
Gamma - 2 c, in the vacuum and in a well alike), the third Rule3's own
rounding of the level, exact in rationals from the step's three levels and
its remainders, and in integers the division act's floor at every Node of
each level pair under one unit beside (the check on the chain by hand: the
floors' part under 10 units of 96 over 400 intervals; `tests/test_the_node.py`).
So the count the line moved was the share one interval ahead of the record,
an integer shadow with two more numbers at every Node that no act of Rule3
read back, and the share is the continuity of Rule3's conserved form
everywhere, the currents its flux.

**The paces' own change moves the share with no current** (the Boss's
finding, #1563 comment 5922238958; the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 00:45,
"not interested in a well"): the identity above holds at the paces the step
read, and a step that moves the paces (a well deepening or filling under a
breathing or moving body) moves the share read at the new paces beside it,
with no current crossing any Link. The numbers: the chain by hand (a breathing
body of matter of 10 Nodes rotating at a sixteenth, the content at its Nodes
330 to 2,330 of Gamma 6,000) reads 66, 128, 56, 227, 50 and 85 quanta over
the board at the intervals 0, 50, 100, 200, 350 and 400; the closed cube of
9^3 reads 157 to 159 over 12 intervals; a static body of the generator on the
chain stays within the rounding over 100. The share stays the count, and its
drift under a moving well is a reading of the books (`drift`, the share's
total against the one the world started with) and no line: the finding of
the moving well stands here by name beside the parked wells, and the well's
three lines, **The count is the baryon number**, **The mass is the well** and

### The click is the meeting

**The click is the meeting of the future with the past** (the owner, 2026-10-01, 00:30 and 04:20, via the advisor, #1563 comments 5922340394 and 5924135189; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the line of the same name; the law and not a hypothesis; replacing the detector's write of the opposite events, No joint read and the click as a whole quantum's entry). A record runs on the board in both directions by the one line: forward from the emitter, the possibilities, the future; backward from the realised click, what happened, the past, Rule3 at -1. A click is where the two carry the same signal; its share is the product of the two directions at the region, the form D = now^2 - next x before, the now against the future times the past; which meeting is realised is the draw, the one non-local place. The backward run brings no new number along the realised path (the board is reversible), carries no count and no signal, and chooses which tree is the past; it is a reading and not a write, and the absorption stays the receding face. The meeting through the root: a pair born as one event is two trees with one root; the backward run from the first click passes the root and continues forward on the partner's tree, by linearity a re-weighting of the pair's parts, and the partner's click meets the re-weighted record; each side alone reads one half at every port, and nothing is signalled. Why the inverse in time is required (the owner: this is the explanation of why reversibility in time is required): the click is the meeting of the two runs, so without the bit-exact inverse the past could not be read back from a click and a pair's root could not be reached; the reversibility of the board is the law's own reason and no convenience of the engine. Status: the owner's declaration, the model's postulate; fence: clicks.

**The one form** (the owner, 04:20, "one form for the click, and it is the meeting; all the rest are cases of it; one form, one draw, one non-local place"): at the credit the instrument reads the record's parts at its port with its coefficients, pairs each part against the same part through the root with what was realised, sums over the parts and squares; the share is that density, and the draw credits one whole quantum by the shares. **The click's algebraic definition** (the advisor, #1563 comment 5924966427, at the owner's word of 06:45; confirmed by the mathematician with five precisions, #1572 comment 5925374010). The objects: the state space X, per Node and per part of every family one integer level pair (now, before) with Rule3's remainder and the write remainders, nothing else; the step S_t, Rule3 at the interval t with the paces read from the held rows, a bijection of X exact by the division with its remainder, affine in the levels at fixed paces and, with the hold reading the rows the records' forms source, a bijection and not a linear map, so the group the steps generate with the lattice's translations, the 48 rotations, the time reversal and the plane's rational rotation is a group of bijections; its forward orbit x_(t+1) = S_t(x_t) is the future and its inverse orbit the past; the readings, bilinear forms on X never written, D = <now, now> - <next, before>, F_ij = num (now_i before_j - before_i now_j) and W for a plane, covariant under the group: D even under the time reversal, F and W odd (a current and a sense flip), so the click, forward only, is the one act outside the reversal; an instrument R, a declared region with front Ports and coefficients e, defining a quadratic form Q_R on X: for one line the inflow through its front Ports accumulated over the window, SUM over the window of SUM over the front Ports of F, equal to the share that crossed the front (the share's identity at standing paces), non-negative over the whole passage of one forward packet and negative over a cut window or at a dephased edge by a bounded amount (**The share's sign and the credit's floor** below); for a pair the window's sum of the squared cross-side sum over the parts, J, on both members of the level pair, a sum of squares. **A click is the pair (R, t): the credit of one whole quantum to the instrument R at the interval t, drawn with the probability s_R / SUM s, where the share s_R = Q_R(x_t) / W_c is the value of the instrument's quadratic form on the forward orbit over the quantum's measure of a current, W_c = 3 den T.** In the meeting's words: the inverse orbit from the credited state coincides with the forward orbit on the realised path (bijectivity: the past is a reading), and the share is the bilinear form's value at the coincidence, the forward against the backward, which is D at the instrument; for a pair the form pairs each part with the same part through the root before the square. The click writes nothing to X and is no element of the group; it is the one map in the system that is not a bijection, from the vector of shares to a unit vector, the one projection, where the draw lives; it is a scalar under the group (the translations, the rotations and the time reversal leave Q_R), so no click names a Node. The credit's interval is the draw's by the inflow's time profile within the window, the instrument's declaration, the law giving the profile. A frozen Node is dark: with every Link pace 0, R_a = 0, no current crosses its Links and it stands in no instrument's front; its share is not a number and it is never clicked, derived; under the composed paces no content within the width's reach freezes a Node, a collapse ending in a frozen clock whose clicks are slowed as e^(-U) and never stopped (**The paces compose**, [the paces](#the-paces)), so the horizon that clicked nowhere was the quadratic forms'. Every observable is Q_R / W_c on the forward orbit or a ratio of two such, and nothing else is observable (**Every formula is fenced**, HIGHLIGHTS.md: a formula of Q_R / W_c is of the world of clicks; the levels, D, F, W, the share e_i, the paces, the bands and their coefficients are of the GameBoard, diagnostics; the file's numbers and the instrument's declarations, the region, the window, the floor, the settings, are declared, a third mark for what is not a formula). **A family's write is one act**, and a held part takes one write (the owner, 2026-10-01, 01:15 and 01:55, HIGHLIGHTS.md **Everything at a Node that is not the generic Node leaves**; the advisor's form, #1538 comment 5922778224): every write into a held part's level on the GameBoard is one division per interval, (SUM over the sourcing families of w_f x q_f(Node) + r) div E at the Node, the division act with the one remainder r in [0, E) carried at that Node and starting at E div 2 (the start's origin): q_f(Node) the writer's own quantity there (a family of quanta's form D_f = now^2 - next x before over its lines for a row sourced by the form, its Wronskian W_f for the holder of the sign, or its tension's part at the Node, -num_f h_a,f, **The tension**), w_f the one weight of the pair of families (whoever reads with w sources with w), and E the part's wall, E_s T for the time part (E_s the row's level weight, the quanta of form that write one level of the row, E_s T the measure of a level as W_c = 3 den T is the quantum's measure of a current; T the universe's quantum action) and E_s W_c for an axis part, W_c = 3 den T with the sourcing families' den (where two sources of one row's axis parts have different den, the least common multiple of their den in den's place and each tension times the multiple over its own den, the same fractions over one wall, exact); the sources are summed in the numerator, never divided apart, so no remainder stands per sourcing family and no well is kept, the well D_i div T a reading; the hold and **The start** (the rest of the hold's line, its fixed point) are its instances; no number is the act's own; since the owner's word of 2026-10-01, 17:02, the write carries the factor of the proper volume and the proper interval, p_x p_y p_z / (p_0 Gamma^2) on the Node's own count and p_x p_y p_z / (p_0^2 Gamma) on the Wronskian, inside the Node's scaled read, by the Node's paces alone and no number (**The write per proper volume and per proper interval**, [the paces](#the-paces); the engine round owed, its booking the three axes' divisions one at a time), and the odd lines' source is the sign's own current, as the engine carries it since PR #1592 (**The odd line's source is the sign's own current**, [the hypotheses under their own names](#the-hypotheses-under-their-own-names)); generic (the rows' keys), vector (one carried division per part), local (the Node).


Every act of the engine is a pure function of the whole GameBoard's
arrays that calls Rule3 and nothing else, in `src/event_universe/node.py`
and the modules beside it (`reports.py`, `growth.py`) and in the folders of
`src/event_universe/features/` (the currents, the
hold, the write, the read, the start); the GameBoard calls them in
the interval's order ([the interval](#the-interval)). No act holds a
number, a family's name or a default. Every write is a whole integer
into a family's level; every division's remainder lives at the Node
where it divided.
